The Competition Association began its activities in 1997, completing its organisation. Competitive areas can be considered under three topics -- implementation of the rules of competition law, competitiveness and policy development. Competitive law rules include implementation, regulation and audit activities. Competitiveness includes public awareness of the benefits of competition with other public institutions and activities aimed at the establishment and development of the competitive environment. Politics development is initiatives aimed at including competitive perspective on economic policies and activities aimed at determining the principles of the institution by guiding its practices. With these activities, the institution has helped to remove barriers to free trade and develop competition in the markets. The existence of competitive markets contributes to increased consumer well-being, economic growth, price stability, increase of foreign investments, fair revenue distribution and increased international competitiveness. With the modernisation of this minor law, increasing the efficiency of the institution is an important requirement. As a matter of fact, today's digitalisation, rapidly changing markets, and the setbacks in these markets require a more efficient and rapid decision for the establishment of competition. At the same time, our economy will be protected from the cumbersomeness of market failure and ensure increased consumer well-being. Dear friends, this bill is a technical law, a 16-point proposal, including administrative procedure. The first amendment of the proposed law aims to clarify the method of self-evaluation and thus increase legal clarity in the immunity regime. This arrangement will allow attempts to move faster in this area, free them from unnecessary transactions and time costs. With the amendment, the competition board's exclusive authority in this area has been revoked, while the initiatives have been granted the right to an application to the Institution optionally. As you know, in particular, in 1994, data were not held or kept in electronics. When we made this arrangement, we kept the scope too narrow. We can safely say that regulations in the European Union are far ahead of these regulations. The law proposal brings 3 new vehicles, in line with European Union legislation, to ensure that public resources can be used more efficiently, to make competitive law more effective. Thus, it is intended that the Association of Competitiveness can focus on violations that have more impact on consumer well-being. The first of these tools is to ensure that non-investigative practices do not be investigated and that the Competitive Institute is focused on violations that have more impact on consumer well-being. The commitment of one of the vehicles within this scope will allow the long investigation processes of the law to be terminated without any need for a competitive concern. In this way, competitive concerns will be resolved using much less public resources and initiatives will be spared the costs that long investigation processes can lead to. The third of these vehicles, the plea of reconciliation, is a procedure for attempts to recognize and accept the violation that he has committed, which is also required by the market. The initiatives will both qualify for a reduction in administrative fines and will save transaction costs due to the rapid end of investigation processes. In addition to these arrangements, dear friends, we have included some regulations in the law proposal to make the management of the Competitive Institution more effective. In this framework, the Council's mandate to abort the staff and commit is laid down on the condition that the basic rules of staff degrees and numbers of which have been revoked by the Constitutional Court should be regulated openly in the law. tbmm-2020-06-11sit03spe0181par0021	Mr. President, dear lawmakers, I have made a pledge on behalf of the PARTI Group on the first chapter of the Law Proposal on the Protection of Competitiveness. I salute your high council. Competition is a mandatory condition of the market economy, and the market economy is based on the principle of competition. A free-competitive economy provides effective use of resources as well as lower prices of competing goods, boosting the quality of businesses that want to have a bigger share in the market. The concept of competition under Article 3 of the Law on the Protection of Competitiveness, 1994, is defined as the race that allows free economic decisions to be made between attempts in the markets of goods and services. However, the right of competition, which allows them to freely decide on initiatives, has forced public authorities to intervene in this situation, by restricting the competition in order to compete with each other, in order to compete with each other more, by restricting competition in the market. The state is the most important factor of the economy as an institution that makes the arrangements in time to ensure the healthy functioning of the markets and monitors them instantly. The state carried out its mission in 1994, with the adoption of the Law on the Protection of Competition 4054 and the establishment of the Competitive Institution, which was the authority to enforce this law in 1997. The Competitive Association, established to prevent competitiveness, disruptive or restrictive agreements, decision-making and practices, and attempts to dominate the market, and to ensure that competition is protected by the necessary regulations and inspections. No body, authority, authority and person can give orders and instructions to influence the institution's final decision. The Competitiveness Agency, which is the enforcer of competition law, has administrative sanctions, was founded by the lawmaker with a structure largely independent of political authority so that he can stay out of the influence of politics. The application is linked to the structure of the regulatory institution, whether public benefit will be observed or not. The regulatory institution, independent of politics and the market it organises, but also accountable in its structure, will be possible to maintain public interest. However, recent legal regulations threaten the independence of the institution. The institution's governing body, the Competition Council, has increased the number of members appointed by political authority. It is clear that decisions made by the political authority of the board members and can be removed from office will not be favourable by the political market economy or the free market economy or foreign investors. Members of parliament, the state has a fundamental duty to prevent unfair competition and protect consumers as well as to regulate competitive conditions. We've seen the latest example of this in the early days of the Colorado outbreak in our country. Let's remember what happened: mask prices rose by 600 to 700 percent, as did disinfectant and cologne prices. Let's skip that, because suddenly our citizens are in the market, even the price of pasta has gone up. In this case, the Competition Board has rightly stepped in and launched an investigation into 10 attempts in the production and sale of medical, protective and fabric masks in the process of the coronavirus epidemic.

In line with the third joke of the 101st Article of the Mehaz Treaty of the European Union in our country, the system of immunity, which has been introduced with Article 5 of the Competitive Law, has been implemented for approximately 23 years. A similar development in the past in Europe has occurred in our country, while the institution has faced the workload of notification files in its first year of operation. Although this burden has decreased over time, there has been a 40-year application for exemption and dissolution. In addition, conditions and obligations have not been able to be placed on a healthy basis in the decisions of exemption and extenuation. Even notifications of group exemptions, which are expected to reduce the number of notifications, have at times had the opposite effect, and initiatives have even applied to the institution to determine if they are under group immunity. In fact, a period of non-existent status has been established by the Council in the form of a conditional group exemption, with decisions being made in order for the agreement to be considered as part of group exemption. [[A round of applause from the good parties]] Dear lawmakers, this clause we're discussing is also authorized to take structural measures to the Competitive Council. These structural measures allow the Competitive Council to ensure that their assets, partnerships, or working arm are dispossessed. Structural measures will be applied if behavioral measures previously implemented fail to yield results. Thus, testing behavioral measures can be perceived as a prerequisite for structural measures. At the same time, the Council's final decision is to report structural measures. In this case, it reveals the possibility that we will now begin to see behavioral and structural measures within each final decision. At this point, there is no clarity as to how to determine the lack of behavioral measures. The decision to move to structural measures is also not granted any additional defences or remarks. The reason for the relevant clause states that structural measures are an exceptional measure that will take effect when behavioral measures fail, which will provide legal security for the initiatives. It is also included in the text of matter that measures should be proportional to violations. Although such legal security has been established, it is unlikely to agree. Because structural measures can have very severe consequences as well as quality and efficiency, and legal security factors are not enough. In particular, the uncertainty of the assessment made during the transition from behavioral measures to structural measures and the lack of defence for attempts at this stage also demonstrates the lack of legal security. This proposed arrangement is also directly related to the 48th article of the Constitution, titled "work and freedom of contract." Article 48 also includes the freedom of working and contracting in the desired area, the freedom to work and not contract in the desired area. When we consider this in terms of competitive law, initiatives can't be forced to work in a field or to sign a contract on a specific matter. In this sense, the emphasis on structural measures in which the proposed amendment text presents specific activities or the transfer of partnership shares or assets is clearly contrary to the basic rights and freedom given in Article 48 of the Constitution. The arrangement, which is said to be a public corporate figure, allows the Competitiveness Agency to make contracts they don't want, to take structural measures that force them to leave the areas they want to work.

Dear lawmakers, although we've been in a digital world, in the process of digitalizing all sorts of data, it seems as if this arrangement is good, but it brings with it a lot of reservations, unfortunately. Competitive Board officials copy any documents, digital data, any information stored in the IT system without any borders or commercial secrets, having access to printouts, is a subject of abuse. In the existing materials, it is possible to get a form of documents and documents, but in the new proposal, each of these data can be obtained with copies of printouts, and there will be no limit to any of these copies. Any data in the physical, electronic and IT system, based on all kinds of digital data reviews, will be copied and taken, regardless of whether it is necessary or not. Such an application has a problem with the safety of the law. In order for the commercial secrets of a business to not be revealed, not to damage the reputation of the business, or to not open the data to circulation in the market, the data must be done in the presence of legal representatives, listed information, data copying procedures. Legal regulations and obligations concerning limiting the transactions to the law, never being used or shared outside the purpose of the copied data must be written openly into the law. In addition, there have been problems with these substances in the business world, which has been passed on to our Commission. tbmm-2020-06-11sit06spe0370par0538	Mr. President, dear lawmakers, I salute our General Assembly. We can ensure that competition is maintained by law and economics together in a healthy way. The regulations and inspections that allow competition to develop in a fair way are undoubtedly in the best interest of our economy, which is not monopoliticized, far from cartelization. Talking about competitive law in market conditions, which are often seen as unplanned, untrustable, unstable, and uncompromising, is nothing more than a sense of perception. In fact, the main factor for the creation of the competitive environment is the removal of our economy from the crisis, which is essentially our democracy, the establishment of a structure that promises stability and trust again. In the economic climate, which feeds on true democracy, growth in both favours of producers and consumers is inherently new in the field of research development. Today, I will continue to share my views on the law proposal, even though I know that discussing these matters is of no use in an environment where certain sectors are divided into families close to the EC PARTY, where arbitrary is considered a rule, where free enterprise and free thought are over-restricted. The right to competition, which allows free enterprise to decide freely, can sometimes be abused. When it is seen that some limited producers are cartelised and monopoliticized to win more in order to compete with each other, it must be inevitable to intervene by the state's hands. No doubt the basis of competition and competition on the basis of the law is vital to all actors in the economy to protect investors' rights. The state, in fact, is the most important rule maker of the economy as a structure that makes arrangements in time to ensure the healthy functioning of the markets and monitors them instantly. In this regard, the competitive institution with the executive sanction of competitive law was founded by the lawmaker with a structure free of political authority, administrative and financial autonomy so that he could stay out of the influence of politics. However, the Competition Association, which began to lose its autonomy in 2011 and 2012, has now become an institution where members of the Council have been appointed by political authority and can be deposed. The decisions made by such an institution are clearly not positive by the free market economy or by foreign investors. The autonomous structure of the Competition Foundation needs to be maintained. In addition, it is essential that the courts, whose expertise is competitive law, be neglected. Precious lawmakers say the proposal aims to make the Competitiveness Foundation Law active and dynamic, to update it and to meet the European Union's legislation and to capture the modern level. New concepts, such as reconciliation and commitment, will be adopted into Turkish competitive law, thus ensuring that a more effective competitive legal system will be established. The clause I have promised is proposed to bring the concept of commitment in the European Union legislation into Turkish competitive law legislation and implementation. There is also an offer that if the commitments made by the initiative or initiative units to address the concerns of competition that have arisen have been accepted by the Council, considering the qualifications and timings of the commitments given by the Union, it may not be able to open an investigation into the initiative or initiative units, or the investigations carried out may be terminated. On the other hand, the concept of reconciliation, which is part of the European Union's legislation and in many countries' implementation, is also envisioned to be introduced to Turkish competitive legislation. Thus, it is intended to shorten the investigation process to a rapid conclusion, as well as to reduce public costs, which will result in lawsuit processes related to them. We discussed the possibility of compromise and commitment mechanism offered to be included in the Competition Law, as well as the proposals we discussed earlier regarding tax regulations. AS A GOOD PARTY, we value the healthy implementation of this arrangement. In the current order, the Competitive Institution must continue to investigate firms until the end and punish companies in accordance with the Penal Code. After our Competition Law came into force, it was intended to end the long and demanding investigation process between the parties, both for competitive institutions and for companies, with compromise and commitment mechanisms adopted by the European Union. The relevant provisions considered to be replaced are also aimed at implementing these mechanisms.

Last week, we, members of the National Movement Party, spoke in the press room one by one about the problems of our citrus manufacturers. Today, our Minister has made a statement in the morning about the problems of the citrus manufacturers. We see that this statement from our Minister is positive, but there are some parts that we see missing, and I particularly want to share them here. Mr. Secretary has stated that such a study has been carried out, especially in regards to the addition of extreme temperatures to the TARSIM coverage next year. Our manufacturers see it as really positive. Because the extreme heat is not assessed within the scope of TARSIM, nor does TARSEM cover the damage caused by extreme heat.

tbmm-2020-06-11sit07spe0391par0114	Mr. President, Mr. Deputys, I have made a pledge on the 11th Amendment. In the existing legislation, the Council's president and members were not subject to Law Code 4483 for their crimes in connection with their duties, but the new proposal changed; thus the assurance that the head of the institution, members and staff would be autonomous has been eliminated. Members of the board have been considered public officials, and the provisions of Article 104 of the Banking Law shall be carried out in terms of criminal and legal responsibility. The launch of an investigation into the president and members of the board is now subject to the Ministry's permission. This will lead to the institution's perception and implementation that it is governed by an understanding that it is acting under political directives, which is committed to power. So, right now, we're saying that the most important structure of this Council needs to be autonomous, and the members of the board are also facing a threat of judgment on the other hand. So it's not enough that all members of the government are appointed by one man who doesn't do what the government says, who's not doing what the government wants, who's not doing what the government wants, who's still resisting, who, despite what the government says, are taking any kind of stand against it, and this time, it's being brought to trial by the Ministry's permission. So this situation, as a whole, will wipe out the autonomous structure and independent structure of the Competitive Council and will be undisputed. Thus, the institution wants to be transformed into a typical public institution. Thus, the institution's founding philosophy will be eliminated. Earlier, the Council of the Supreme Court, Prosecutor's Office, Turkey's Association of Chambers and Stock Exchanges, the Ministry of Development and the Ministry of Customs and Trade could make recommendations in the process of appointing members. The board was originally composed of 11 people. In 2005, the number was reduced to 7 ▪ and all members are currently assigned to the appointment of one person. The Supreme Court, Prosecutorate, Turkey's Association of Chambers and Stock Exchanges, appointed by the Ministry of Development and the Ministry of Customs and Trade to appoint members before 2018, was the decision-making body of the Competitive Institution in 2018, and it is no longer possible to talk about the autonomy and independence of the institution, as one of the presidents appointed by the president. We know that especially during this period of power, the judiciary is completely under the control of power and has become an institution governed and directed by its instructions. The court's decisions have been repeatedly discussed and explained from this bench, and its examples have been explained -- even though the judge is released by the trial, even if the judge gives a decision of freedom immediately after which he has been warned, the decision was reversed by the prosecutor's objection, and the judges who made that decision are being driven elsewhere. That's what's happened, and with many examples, especially the politicians of our party, the MPs, they've been exposed to it a lot, and they continue to stay. In this sense, along with this clause, there is a threat of judicial action on these members of the Council, which is intended to be created. This is the essence and content of the 11th article. I salute the General Assembly.
